<div class="col-sm order-1"><h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 363A. HUMAN RIGHTS</h2><div class="sr" id="stat.363A.001"><b>363A.001</b> MS 2006 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.001">15.001</a>]</div><h1 class="fgh">GENERAL PROVISIONS</h1><div class="section" id="stat.363A.01"><h1 class="shn">363A.01 CITATION.</h1><p>This chapter shall be known as the "Minnesota Human Rights Act."</p></div><div class="history" id="stat.363A.01.history
			"><h2>History: </h2><p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/516/#laws.0.2.0">1955 c 516 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/428/#laws.0.17.0">1961 c 428 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/729/#laws.0.17.0">1973 c 729 s 17</a></p></div><div class="section" id="stat.363A.02"><h1 class="shn">363A.02 PUBLIC POLICY.</h1><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.02.1"><a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.02.1">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Freedom from discrimination.</span></h2><p>(a) It is the public policy of this state to secure for persons in this state, freedom from discrimination:</p><p>(1) in employment because of one or more of the following: race, color, creed, religion, national origin, sex, gender identity, marital status, disability, status with regard to public assistance, sexual orientation, familial status, and age;</p><p>(2) in housing and real property because of one or more of the following: race, color, creed, religion, national origin, sex, gender identity, marital status, disability, status with regard to public assistance, sexual orientation, and familial status;</p><p>(3) in public accommodations because of one or more of the following: race, color, creed, religion, national origin, sex, gender identity, sexual orientation, and disability;</p><p>(4) in public services because of one or more of the following: race, color, creed, religion, national origin, sex, gender identity, marital status, disability, sexual orientation, and status with regard to public assistance; and</p><p>(5) in education because of one or more of the following: race, color, creed, religion, national origin, sex, gender identity, marital status, disability, status with regard to public assistance, sexual orientation, and age.</p><p>(b) Such discrimination threatens the rights and privileges of the inhabitants of this state and menaces the institutions and foundations of democracy. It is also the public policy of this state to protect all persons from wholly unfounded charges of discrimination. Nothing in this chapter shall be interpreted as restricting the implementation of positive action programs to combat discrimination.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.02.2"><a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.02.2">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Civil right.</span></h2><p>The opportunity to obtain employment, housing, and other real estate, and full and equal utilization of public accommodations, public services, and educational institutions without such discrimination as is prohibited by this chapter is hereby recognized as and declared to be a civil right.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.02.3"><a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.02.3">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Severability.</span></h2><p>If any provision of Laws 1967, chapter 897, or the application thereof to any person or circumstances is held invalid, the invalidity does not affect the other provisions or applications of Laws 1967, chapter 897, which can be given effect without the invalid provision or application, and to this end the provisions of Laws 1967, chapter 897, are severable.</p></div></div><div class="history" id="stat.363A.02.history
			"><h2>History: </h2><p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/516/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 516 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/428/#laws.0.16.0">1961 c 428 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/897/#laws.0.26.0">1967 c 897 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/975/#laws.0.15.0">1969 c 975 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/729/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 729 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/351/#laws.0.11.0">1977 c 351 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/531/#laws.0.8.0">1980 c 531 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/22/#laws.0.19.0">1993 c 22 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/11/#laws.3.12.0">1Sp2021 c 11 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.19.45.0">2023 c 52 art 19 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/105/#laws.0.1.0">2024 c 105 s 1</a></p></div><div class="section" id="stat.363A.03"><h1 class="shn">363A.03 DEFINITIONS.</h1><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.1"><a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.1">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Terms.</span></h2><p>For the purposes of this chapter, the words defined in this section have the meanings ascribed to them.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.2"><a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.2">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Age.</span></h2><p>The prohibition against unfair employment or education practices based on age prohibits using a person's age as a basis for a decision if the person is over the age of majority except for section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.13">363A.13</a> which shall be deemed to protect any individual over the age of 25 years.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.3"><a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.3">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2><p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/105/#laws.0.20.0">2024 c 105 s 20</a>]</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.4"><a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.4">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Business.</span></h2><p>The term "business" includes any partnership, association, corporation, legal representative, trustee, trustee in bankruptcy, or receiver, but excludes the state and its departments, agencies, and political subdivisions.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.5"><a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.5">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Charging party.</span></h2><p>"Charging party" means a person filing a charge with the commissioner or the commissioner's designated agent pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.28#stat.363A.28.1">363A.28, subdivision 1</a>.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.6"><a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.6">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Closed case file.</span></h2><p>"Closed case file" means a file containing human rights investigative data in which an order or other decision resolving the alleged or suspected discrimination has been made or issued by the commissioner, a hearing officer, or a court, and the time for any reconsideration of or appeal from the order or decision has expired.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.7"><a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.7">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2><p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of human rights.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.8"><a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.8">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Complainant.</span></h2><p>"Complainant" means the commissioner of human rights after issuing a complaint pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.06#stat.363A.06.3">363A.06, subdivision 3, paragraph (8)</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.28">363A.28</a>, subdivisions 1 to 9.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.9"><a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.9">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Confidential, private, and public data on individuals and protected nonpublic data not on individuals.</span></h2><p>"Confidential," "private," "public data on individuals," "protected nonpublic data not on individuals," and any other terms concerning the availability of human rights investigative data have the meanings given them by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02">13.02</a> of the Minnesota Government Data Practices Act.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.10"><a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.10">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Demand responsive system.</span></h2><p>"Demand responsive system" means a system of providing public transportation that is not a fixed route system.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.11"><a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.11">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Department.</span></h2><p>"Department" means the Department of Human Rights.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.12"><a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.12">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Disability.</span></h2><p>"Disability" means any condition or characteristic that renders a person a disabled person. A disabled person is any person who (1) has a physical, sensory, or mental impairment which materially limits one or more major life activities; (2) has a record of such an impairment; (3) is regarded as having such an impairment; or (4) has an impairment that is episodic or in remission and would materially limit a major life activity when active.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.13"><a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.13">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Discriminate.</span></h2><p>The term "discriminate" includes segregate, separate, or harassment.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.14"><a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.14">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Educational institution.</span></h2><p>"Educational institution" means a public or private institution and includes an academy, college, elementary or secondary school, extension course, kindergarten, nursery, school system and a business, nursing, professional, secretarial, technical, vocational school, and includes an agent of an educational institution.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.15"><a title="Link to Subd. 15." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.15">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Employee.</span></h2><p>"Employee" means an individual who is employed by an employer and who resides or works in this state. Employee includes a commission salesperson, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/181.145">181.145</a>, who resides or works in this state.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.16"><a title="Link to Subd. 16." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.16">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Employer.</span></h2><p>"Employer" means a person who has one or more employees.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.17"><a title="Link to Subd. 17." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.17">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Employment agency.</span></h2><p>"Employment agency" means a person or persons who, or an agency which regularly undertakes, with or without compensation, to procure employees or opportunities for employment.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.18"><a title="Link to Subd. 18." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.18">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Familial status.</span></h2><p>"Familial status" means the condition of one or more minors having legal status or custody with (1) the minor's parent or parents or the minor's legal guardian or guardians or (2) the designee of the parent or parents or guardian or guardians with the written permission of the parent or parents or guardian or guardians. Familial status also means residing with and caring for one or more individuals who lack the ability to meet essential requirements for physical health, safety, or self-care because the individual or individuals are unable to receive and evaluate information or make or communicate decisions. The protections afforded against discrimination on the basis of family status apply to any person who is pregnant or is in the process of securing legal custody of an individual who has not attained the age of majority.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.19"><a title="Link to Subd. 19." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.19">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Fixed route system.</span></h2><p>"Fixed route system" means a system of providing public transportation on which a vehicle is operated along a prescribed route according to a fixed schedule.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.20"><a title="Link to Subd. 20." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.20">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Historic or antiquated rail passenger car.</span></h2><p>"Historic or antiquated rail passenger car" means a rail passenger car:</p><p>(1) that is at least 30 years old at the time of its use for transporting individuals;</p><p>(2) the manufacturer of which is no longer in the business of manufacturing rail passenger cars; or</p><p>(3) that has consequential association with events or persons significant to the past or embodies, or is being restored to embody, the distinctive characteristics of a type of rail passenger car used in the past or to represent a time period that has passed.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.21"><a title="Link to Subd. 21." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.21">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Human rights investigative data.</span></h2><p>"Human rights investigative data" means written documents issued or gathered by the department for the purpose of investigating and prosecuting alleged or suspected discrimination.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.22"><a title="Link to Subd. 22." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.22">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Labor organization.</span></h2><p>"Labor organization" means any organization that exists wholly or partly for one or more of the following purposes:</p><p>(1) collective bargaining;</p><p>(2) dealing with employers concerning grievances, terms or conditions of employment; or</p><p>(3) mutual aid or protection of employees.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.23"><a title="Link to Subd. 23." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.23">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Local commission.</span></h2><p>"Local commission" means an agency of a city, county, or group of counties created pursuant to law, resolution of a county board, city charter, or municipal ordinance for the purpose of dealing with discrimination on the basis of race, color, creed, religion, national origin, sex, gender identity, age, disability, marital status, status with regard to public assistance, sexual orientation, or familial status.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.24"><a title="Link to Subd. 24." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.24">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Marital status.</span></h2><p>"Marital status" means whether a person is single, married, remarried, divorced, separated, or a surviving spouse and, in employment cases, includes protection against discrimination on the basis of the identity, situation, actions, or beliefs of a spouse or former spouse.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.25"><a title="Link to Subd. 25." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.25">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">National origin.</span></h2><p>"National origin" means the place of birth of an individual or of any of the individual's lineal ancestors.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.26"><a title="Link to Subd. 26." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.26">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">Open case file.</span></h2><p>"Open case file" means a file containing human rights investigative data in which no order or other decision resolving the alleged or suspected discrimination has been made or issued by the commissioner, a hearing officer, or a court, or a file in which an order or other decision has been issued but the time for any reconsideration or appeal of the order or decision has either not yet expired or the reconsideration or appeal is then pending.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.27"><a title="Link to Subd. 27." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.27">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Operates.</span></h2><p>"Operates," when used with respect to a demand responsive or fixed route system, includes the operation of the system by a person under a contractual or other arrangement or relationship with a public or private entity.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.28"><a title="Link to Subd. 28." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.28">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Over-the-road bus.</span></h2><p>"Over-the-road bus" means a bus characterized by an elevated passenger deck located over a baggage compartment.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.29"><a title="Link to Subd. 29." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.29">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Party in interest.</span></h2><p>"Party in interest" means the complainant, respondent, or commissioner.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.30"><a title="Link to Subd. 30." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.30">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2><p>"Person" includes partnership, association, corporation, legal representative, trustee, trustee in bankruptcy, receiver, and the state and its departments, agencies, and political subdivisions.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.31"><a title="Link to Subd. 31." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.31">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Physical access.</span></h2><p>"Physical access" means (1) the absence of physical obstacles that limit a disabled person's opportunity for full and equal use of or benefit from goods, services, and privileges; or, when necessary, (2) the use of methods to overcome the discriminatory effect of physical obstacles. The methods may include redesign of equipment, assignment of aides, or use of alternate accessible locations.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.32"><a title="Link to Subd. 32." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.32">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Private entity.</span></h2><p>"Private entity" means an entity other than a public service.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.33"><a title="Link to Subd. 33." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.33">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.<span class="headnote">Program access.</span></h2><p>"Program access" means (1) the use of auxiliary aids or services to ensure full and equal use of or benefit from goods, services, and privileges; and (2) the absence of criteria or methods of administration that directly, indirectly, or through contractual or other arrangements, have the effect of subjecting qualified disabled persons to discrimination on the basis of disability, or have the effect of defeating or impairing the accomplishment of the objectives of the program.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.34"><a title="Link to Subd. 34." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.34">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Place of public accommodation.</span></h2><p>"Place of public accommodation" means a business, accommodation, refreshment, entertainment, recreation, or transportation facility of any kind, whether licensed or not, whose goods, services, facilities, privileges, advantages or accommodations are extended, offered, sold, or otherwise made available to the public.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.35"><a title="Link to Subd. 35." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.35">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.<span class="headnote">Public service.</span></h2><p>"Public service" means any public facility, department, agency, board or commission, owned, operated or managed by or on behalf of the state of Minnesota, or any subdivision thereof, including any county, city, town, township, or independent district in the state.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.36"><a title="Link to Subd. 36." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.36">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Qualified disabled person.</span></h2><p>"Qualified disabled person" means:</p><p>(1) with respect to employment, a disabled person who, with reasonable accommodation, can perform the essential functions required of all applicants for the job in question; and</p><p>(2) with respect to public services, a person with a disability who, with or without reasonable modifications to rules, policies, or practices, removal of architectural, communications, or transportation barriers, or the provision of auxiliary aids and services, meets the essential eligibility requirements for receipt of services and for participation in programs and activities provided by the public service.</p><p>For the purposes of this subdivision, "disability" excludes any condition resulting from alcohol or drug abuse which prevents a person from performing the essential functions of the job in question or constitutes a direct threat to property or the safety of others.</p><p>If a respondent contends that the person is not a qualified disabled person, the burden is on the respondent to prove that it was reasonable to conclude the disabled person, with reasonable accommodation, could not have met the requirements of the job or that the selected person was demonstrably better able to perform the job.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.36a"><a title="Link to Subd. 36a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.36a">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36a.<span class="headnote">Race.</span></h2><p>"Race" is inclusive of traits associated with race, including but not limited to hair texture and hair styles such as braids, locs, and twists.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.37"><a title="Link to Subd. 37." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.37">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">Rail passenger car.</span></h2><p>"Rail passenger car" means, with respect to intercity or commuter rail transportation, single- and bi-level coach cars, dining cars, sleeping cars, lounge cars, restroom cars, and food service cars.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.38"><a title="Link to Subd. 38." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.38">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.<span class="headnote">Real estate broker or salesperson.</span></h2><p>"Real estate broker or salesperson" means, respectively, a real estate broker as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/82.55">82.55</a>, subdivision 19, and a real estate salesperson as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/82.55">82.55</a>, subdivision 20.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.39"><a title="Link to Subd. 39." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.39">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Real property.</span></h2><p>"Real property" includes real estate, lands, tenements, and hereditaments, corporeal and incorporeal.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.40"><a title="Link to Subd. 40." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.40">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Religious or denominational educational institution.</span></h2><p>"Religious or denominational educational institution" means an educational institution which is operated, supervised, controlled or sustained primarily by a religious or denominational organization, or one which is stated by the parent church body to be and is, in fact, officially related to that church by being represented on the board of the institution, and by providing substantial financial assistance and which has certified, in writing, to the board that it is a religious or denominational educational institution.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.41"><a title="Link to Subd. 41." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.41">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.<span class="headnote">Respondent.</span></h2><p>"Respondent" means a person against whom a complaint has been filed or issued.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.42"><a title="Link to Subd. 42." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.42">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.<span class="headnote">Sex.</span></h2><p>"Sex" includes, but is not limited to, pregnancy, childbirth, and disabilities related to pregnancy or childbirth.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.43"><a title="Link to Subd. 43." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.43">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.<span class="headnote">Sexual harassment.</span></h2><p>"Sexual harassment" includes unwelcome sexual advances, requests for sexual favors, sexually motivated physical contact or other verbal or physical conduct or communication of a sexual nature when:</p><p>(1) submission to that conduct or communication is made a term or condition, either explicitly or implicitly, of obtaining employment, public accommodations or public services, education, or housing;</p><p>(2) submission to or rejection of that conduct or communication by an individual is used as a factor in decisions affecting that individual's employment, public accommodations or public services, education, or housing; or</p><p>(3) that conduct or communication has the purpose or effect of substantially interfering with an individual's employment, public accommodations or public services, education, or housing, or creating an intimidating, hostile, or offensive employment, public accommodations, public services, educational, or housing environment.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.44"><a title="Link to Subd. 44." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.44">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 44.<span class="headnote">Sexual orientation.</span></h2><p>"Sexual orientation" means to whom someone is, or is perceived of as being, emotionally, physically, or sexually attracted to based on sex or gender identity. A person may be attracted to men, women, both, neither, or to people who are genderqueer, androgynous, or have other gender identities.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.45"><a title="Link to Subd. 45." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.45">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45.<span class="headnote">Specified public transportation.</span></h2><p>"Specified public transportation" means transportation by bus, rail, or any other conveyance other than aircraft that provides the general public with general or special service, including charter service, on a regular and continuing basis.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.46"><a title="Link to Subd. 46." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.46">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 46.<span class="headnote">Station.</span></h2><p>"Station" means property located next to a right-of-way on which intercity and commuter transportation is operated, which is used by the general public and is related to the provision of the transportation, including passenger platforms, designated waiting areas, ticketing areas, restrooms, drinking fountains, public telephones, and, if a public service providing rail transportation owns the property, concessions areas to the extent that the public service exercises control over the selection, design, construction, or alteration of the property. Station does not include flag stops.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.47"><a title="Link to Subd. 47." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.47">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 47.<span class="headnote">Status with regard to public assistance.</span></h2><p>"Status with regard to public assistance" means the condition of being a recipient of federal, state, or local assistance, including medical assistance, or of being a tenant receiving federal, state, or local subsidies, including rental assistance or rent supplements.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.48"><a title="Link to Subd. 48." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.48">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48.<span class="headnote">Unfair discriminatory practice.</span></h2><p>"Unfair discriminatory practice" means any act described in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.08">363A.08</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.19">363A.19</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.28#stat.363A.28.10">363A.28, subdivision 10</a>.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.49"><a title="Link to Subd. 49." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.49">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 49.<span class="headnote">Vehicle.</span></h2><p>"Vehicle" does not include a rail passenger car, railroad locomotive, railroad freight car, railroad caboose, or railroad car.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.03.50"><a title="Link to Subd. 50." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.03.50">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 50.<span class="headnote">Gender identity.</span></h2><p>"Gender identity" means a person's inherent sense of being a man, woman, both, or neither. A person's gender identity may or may not correspond to their assigned sex at birth or to their primary or secondary sex characteristics. A person's gender identity is not necessarily visible to others.</p></div></div><div class="history" id="stat.363A.03.history
			"><h2>History: </h2><p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/516/#laws.0.3.0">1955 c 516 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/428/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 428 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/897/#laws.0.1.0">1967 c 897 s 1</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/975/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 975 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/729/#laws.0.1.0">1973 c 729 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/2/#laws.0.130.0">1976 c 2 s 130</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/351/#laws.0.1.0">1977 c 351 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/408/#laws.0.1.0">1977 c 408 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/531/#laws.0.1.0">1980 c 531 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/492/#laws.0.1.0">1982 c 492 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/619/#laws.0.2.0">1982 c 619 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/276/#laws.0.1.0">1983 c 276 s 1</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.320.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 320</a>-324; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/23/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 23 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/282/#laws.0.2.0">1987 c 282 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/660/#laws.0.1.0">1988 c 660 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/144/#laws.2.8.0">1989 c 144 art 2 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/280/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 280 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.9.26.0">1989 c 329 art 9 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.243.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 243</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/356/#laws.0.18.0">1989 c 356 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/567/#laws.0.1.0">1990 c 567 s 1</a>,10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/527/#laws.0.1.0">1992 c 527 s 1</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/22/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 22 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/277/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 277 s 1</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/465/#laws.3.20.0">1994 c 465 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/194/#laws.0.1.0">2001 c 194 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/203/#laws.2.61.0">2004 c 203 art 2 s 61</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/3/#laws.0.1.0">2023 c 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.19.46.0">2023 c 52 art 19 s 46</a>-48; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/105/#laws.0.2.0">2024 c 105 s 2</a>-5</p></div><div class="section" id="stat.363A.04"><h1 class="shn">363A.04 CONSTRUCTION AND EXCLUSIVITY.</h1><p>The provisions of this chapter shall be construed liberally for the accomplishment of the purposes thereof. Nothing contained in this chapter shall be deemed to repeal any of the provisions of the civil rights law or of any other law of this state relating to discrimination because of race, creed, color, religion, sex, gender identity, age, disability, marital status, status with regard to public assistance, national origin, sexual orientation, or familial status; but, as to acts declared unfair by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.08">363A.08</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.19">363A.19</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.28#stat.363A.28.10">363A.28, subdivision 10</a>, the administrative procedure herein provided shall, while pending, be exclusive. The rights and remedies herein provided are in addition to, and shall not preclude, those available at law or in equity.</p></div><div class="history" id="stat.363A.04.history
			"><h2>History: </h2><p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/516/#laws.0.13.0">1955 c 516 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/729/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 729 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/351/#laws.0.9.0">1977 c 351 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/531/#laws.0.6.0">1980 c 531 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/280/#laws.0.21.0">1989 c 280 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/22/#laws.0.17.0">1993 c 22 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.19.49.0">2023 c 52 art 19 s 49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/105/#laws.0.6.0">2024 c 105 s 6</a></p></div><div class="section" id="stat.363A.05"><h1 class="shn">363A.05 DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN RIGHTS.</h1><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.05.1"><a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.05.1">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Creation; commissioner.</span></h2><p>There is established a Department of Human Rights under the direction and supervision of a commissioner who shall be appointed by the governor under the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.06">15.06</a>.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.05.2"><a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.05.2">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Deputy commissioner, duties.</span></h2><p>There shall be in the department a deputy commissioner, who shall be appointed by the commissioner and shall serve at the pleasure of the commissioner. The deputy commissioner shall act for, and exercise the powers of the commissioner during the absence or disability of the commissioner or in the event of a vacancy in the office of commissioner. The deputy commissioner shall perform such functions, powers and duties as the commissioner shall prescribe from time to time.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.05.3"><a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.05.3">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Task force, membership, appeals.</span></h2><p>The commissioner may appoint a Human Rights Advisory Task Force.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.05.4"><a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.05.4">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Terms; compensation; removal; vacancies.</span></h2><p>The expiration, membership terms, compensation, removal of members, and filling of vacancies on the task force shall be as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.059">15.059</a>.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.05.5"><a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.05.5">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Departmental organization.</span></h2><p>Subject to other provisions of this chapter, the commissioner shall have the powers granted by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.06">15.06</a> to organize the department.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.05.6"><a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.05.6">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Continuity in operations.</span></h2><p>In exercising the functions, powers and duties conferred on and transferred to the commissioner by Laws 1967, chapter 897, the commissioner shall give full consideration to the need for operational continuity of the functions transferred.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.05.7"><a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.05.7">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Successor agency.</span></h2><p>The Department of Human Rights under the control of the commissioner of human rights is the successor of the State Commission Against Discrimination as it existed immediately prior to July 1, 1967.</p></div></div><div class="history" id="stat.363A.05.history
			"><h2>History: </h2><p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/516/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 516 s 1</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/428/#laws.0.6.0">1961 c 428 s 6</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/586/#laws.0.2.0">1965 c 586 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/897/#laws.0.17.0">1967 c 897 s 17</a>,26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/975/#laws.0.6.0">1969 c 975 s 6</a>,7,15,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1129/#laws.8.14.0">1969 c 1129 art 8 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/729/#laws.0.4.0">1973 c 729 s 4</a>,14,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/134/#laws.0.68.0">1976 c 134 s 68</a>,69; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/305/#laws.0.38.0">1977 c 305 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/351/#laws.0.11.0">1977 c 351 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/444/#laws.0.17.0">1977 c 444 s 17</a>-19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/531/#laws.0.8.0">1980 c 531 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/260/#laws.0.60.0">1983 c 260 s 60</a>,61; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/22/#laws.0.19.0">1993 c 22 s 19</a></p></div><div class="section" id="stat.363A.06"><h1 class="shn">363A.06 POWERS AND DUTIES OF COMMISSIONER.</h1><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.06.1"><a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.06.1">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Formulation of policies.</span></h2><p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall formulate policies to effectuate the purposes of this chapter and shall do the following:</p><p class="in">(1) exercise leadership under the direction of the governor in the development of human rights policies and programs, and make recommendations to the governor and the legislature for their consideration and implementation;</p><p class="in">(2) establish and maintain a principal office in St. Paul, and any other necessary branch offices at any location within the state;</p><p class="in">(3) meet and function at any place within the state;</p><p class="in">(4) employ attorneys, clerks, and other employees and agents as the commissioner may deem necessary and prescribe their duties;</p><p class="in">(5) to the extent permitted by federal law and regulation, utilize the records of the Department of Employment and Economic Development of the state when necessary to effectuate the purposes of this chapter;</p><p class="in">(6) obtain upon request and utilize the services of all state governmental departments and agencies;</p><p class="in">(7) adopt suitable rules for effectuating the purposes of this chapter;</p><p class="in">(8) issue complaints, receive and investigate charges alleging unfair discriminatory practices, and determine whether or not probable cause exists for hearing;</p><p class="in">(9) subpoena witnesses, administer oaths, take testimony, and require the production for examination of any books or papers relative to any matter under investigation or in question as the commissioner deems appropriate to carry out the purposes of this chapter;</p><p class="in">(10) attempt, by means of education, conference, conciliation, and persuasion to eliminate unfair discriminatory practices as being contrary to the public policy of the state;</p><p class="in">(11) develop and conduct programs of formal and informal education designed to eliminate discrimination and intergroup conflict by use of educational techniques and programs the commissioner deems necessary;</p><p class="in">(12) make a written report of the activities of the commissioner to the governor each year;</p><p class="in">(13) accept gifts, bequests, grants, or other payments public and private to help finance the activities of the department;</p><p class="in">(14) create such local and statewide advisory committees as will in the commissioner's judgment aid in effectuating the purposes of the Department of Human Rights;</p><p class="in">(15) develop such programs as will aid in determining the compliance throughout the state with the provisions of this chapter, and in the furtherance of such duties, conduct research and study discriminatory practices based upon race, color, creed, religion, national origin, sex, gender identity, age, disability, marital status, status with regard to public assistance, familial status, sexual orientation, or other factors and develop accurate data on the nature and extent of discrimination and other matters as they may affect housing, employment, public accommodations, schools, and other areas of public life;</p><p class="in">(16) develop and disseminate technical assistance to persons subject to the provisions of this chapter, and to agencies and officers of governmental and private agencies;</p><p class="in">(17) provide staff services to such advisory committees as may be created in aid of the functions of the Department of Human Rights;</p><p class="in">(18) make grants in aid to the extent that appropriations are made available for that purpose in aid of carrying out duties and responsibilities;</p><p class="in">(19) cooperate and consult with the commissioner of labor and industry regarding the investigation of violations of, and resolution of complaints regarding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.08#stat.363A.08.7">363A.08, subdivision 7</a>; and</p><p>(20) analyze civil rights trends pursuant to this chapter, including information compiled from community organizations that work directly with historically marginalized communities, and prepare a report each biennium that recommends policy and system changes to reduce and prevent further civil rights incidents across Minnesota. The report shall be provided to the chairs and ranking minority members of the house of representatives and senate committees with jurisdiction over the Department of Human Rights. This report must also be posted on the Department of Human Rights' public website and shared with community organizations that work with historically marginalized communities.</p><p class="in">In performing these duties, the commissioner shall give priority to those duties in clauses (8), (9), and (10) and to the duties in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/363A.36">363A.36</a>.</p><p class="in">(b) All gifts, bequests, grants, or other payments, public and private, accepted under paragraph (a), clause (13), must be deposited in the state treasury and credited to a special account. Money in the account is appropriated to the commissioner of human rights to help finance activities of the department.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.06.2"><a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.06.2">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Service, enforcement, and effect of subpoena.</span></h2><p>(a) Disobedience of a subpoena issued by the commissioner pursuant to subdivision 1 shall be punishable in like manner as a contempt of the district court in proceedings instituted upon application of the commissioner made to the district court of the county where the alleged unfair discriminatory practice in connection with a charge made by a charging party or a complaint filed by the commissioner has occurred or where the respondent resides or has a principal place of business.</p><p>(b) It is not a violation of rights conferred by chapter 13 or any other statute related to the confidentiality of government data for a state agency, statewide system, or political subdivision, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.11">13.02, subdivision 11</a>, to provide data or information under a subpoena issued by the commissioner under this section.</p><p>(c) A subpoena issued under subdivision 1 must be served personally or by mailing a copy of the subpoena, by first class mail, postage prepaid, to the person to be served. The subpoena must include two copies of a notice and acknowledgment of service on a form to be provided by the commissioner, and a return envelope, postage prepaid, addressed to the sender. If acknowledgment of service is not received by the commissioner within 20 days, service is not effective. Unless good cause is shown for not doing so, a court or administrative law judge shall order the payment of the costs of personal service by the person served if the person does not complete and return the notice and acknowledgment of receipt of the subpoena within the time allowed.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.06.3"><a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.06.3">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Mission; efficiency.</span></h2><p>It is part of the department's mission that within the department's resources the commissioner shall endeavor to:</p><p>(1) prevent the waste or unnecessary spending of public money;</p><p>(2) use innovative fiscal and human resource practices to manage the state's resources and operate the department as efficiently as possible;</p><p>(3) coordinate the department's activities wherever appropriate with the activities of other governmental agencies;</p><p>(4) use technology where appropriate to increase agency productivity, improve customer service, increase public access to information about government, and increase public participation in the business of government;</p><p>(5) utilize constructive and cooperative labor-management practices to the extent otherwise required by chapters 43A and 179A;</p><p>(6) report to the legislature on the performance of agency operations and the accomplishment of agency goals in the agency's biennial budget according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.10#stat.16A.10.1">16A.10, subdivision 1</a>; and</p><p>(7) recommend to the legislature appropriate changes in law necessary to carry out the mission and improve the performance of the department.</p></div><div class="subd" id="stat.363A.06.4"><a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/363A/full#stat.363A.06.4">§</a><h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Publication of case account.</span></h2><p>The commissioner may publish an account of a case in which the complaint has been dismissed or the terms of settlement of a case that has been voluntarily resolved with the Department of Human Rights.</p></div></div><div class="history" id="stat.363A.06.history
			"><h2>History: </h2><p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/516/#laws.0.7.0">1955 c 516 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/428/#laws.0.7.0">1961 c 428 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/299/#laws.0.9.0">1967 c 299 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/897/#laws.0.18.0">1967 c 897 s 18</a>,19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/567/#laws.0.3.0">1969 c 567 s 3</a>;
